Why PDH?
Here at PDH, we go beyond healthcare—we provide genuine care that feels like home. Patients trust us because we put their well-being first, offering expert medical services, compassionate staff, and a welcoming environment that makes every visit reassuring.
Because, we at PDH…
Our accessible location, developing facilities, and dedicated specialists ensure you receive the highest and best quality treatment without the hassle. Whether you’re here for a routine check-up or specialized care, PDH is where expertise meets heart—because you deserve nothing less.
Patient Safety
Our commitment is to place patient safety and top-tier medical standards at the forefront of everything we do.
Compassion & Medical Expertise
We deliver expert medical care with the warmth and compassion every patient deserves.
Cutting-Edge Science & Innovation
Your well-being inspires our focus on innovation.
Accessible & Inclusive Care
From Outpatient and Emergency to Inpatient and Urgent Care Services, we offer a comprehensive range of healthcare solutions tailored to your needs.
Community-Centered Service

Our Network of Care
Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) is a proud member of Metro Pacific Health (MPH), the largest private hospital group in the country.
The 27 hospitals that make up MPH include Makati Medical Center, Cardinal Santos Medical Center, Asian Hospital and Medical Center, Riverside Medical Center, and Davao Doctors Hospital, as well as 33 outpatient care centers, 6 cancer centers, 2 allied health colleges, a centralized laboratory, and several pharmacies across the country – all working together to deliver high-quality compassionate healthcare to Filipinos.
Patient Stories
Our patients’ stories reflect the heart of what we do at PDH—delivering compassionate care, expert treatment, and a healing experience that truly makes a difference. Their journeys inspire us to keep putting patients first, every step of the way.

Importance of Regular Check-ups
Have You Been Hearing About Regular Check-Ups? Here’s Why They’re Essential for Your Health
You’ve probably heard doctors, health talks, events, or even your friends and family emphasize the importance of regular check-ups—but do you really know why they matter? Many people only visit a doctor when they feel sick, but did you know that preventive healthcare is just as crucial?
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we advocate for regular medical check-ups to ensure early detection, better management of health conditions, and overall well-being.

TITLE: 5 Tips for Risk Reduction and Early Detection for Cancer - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: February marks Cancer Awareness Month, a time to shed light on the importance of early detection, prevention, and treatment. At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we are committed to providing top-notch medical care and diagnostic services to help our community stay informed and proactive about their health.
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
5 Tips for Risk Reduction and Early Detection for Cancer
February marks Cancer Awareness Month, a time to shed light on the importance of early detection, prevention, and treatment. At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we are committed to providing top-notch medical care and diagnostic services to help our community stay informed and proactive about their health.
Here are FIVE ESSENTIAL TIPS to help you reduce your risk and detect cancer early:
- Make Regular Check-Ups a Priority Early detection is key to successful treatment. Regular consultations with your doctor and routine screenings—such as mammograms, Pap smears, colonoscopies, and prostate exams—help identify potential health issues before they become serious.
At PDH, we offer comprehensive cancer screening services, including Ultrasound, and CT Scans. Our expert radiologists ensure accurate and timely results.
- Maintain a Healthy Lifestyle A balanced diet and regular exercise play a crucial role in cancer prevention. Consume more fruits, vegetables, and whole grains while reducing processed and red meats.
PDH’s Nutrition and Dietetics provides personalized meal planning and counseling to help you make healthier food choices.
- Avoid Smoking and Excessive Alcohol Consumption Smoking is a leading cause of lung cancer, while excessive alcohol intake is linked to liver, breast, and other cancers. Quitting smoking and drinking in moderation can lower your risk.
- Stay Sun-Safe Skin cancer is one of the most preventable types of cancer. Protect yourself by using sunscreen, wearing protective clothing, and avoiding prolonged sun exposure, especially during peak hours.
If you notice unusual skin changes, PDH’s Dermatology Department is here to provide expert consultations and treatment.
- Know Your Family History A family history of cancer can increase your risk. Inform your doctor about any hereditary conditions so they can recommend genetic counseling and early detection strategies.
PDH’s Oncology Consultations are here to assess hereditary cancer risks and create personalized health plans.
Why Choose Parañaque Doctors Hospital? At PDH, we prioritize your health by providing reliable diagnostic, treatment, and wellness services. Our dedicated team of specialists is here to guide you every step of the way.
Your health is your greatest asset—take action today! For appointments and inquiries, call 8776-0644 to 46, local 2 or visit us at info@pdh.com.ph.

Skip to content
Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) is a premier Level 2 healthcare institution located in the heart of Parañaque City. It is committed to ensuring the well-being of its community by continuously providing high-quality and affordable healthcare services.
Founded on May 8, 2007, by visionary doctors, PDH officially became a member of the Metro Pacific Health (MPH) Group in 2024 — the largest Philippine private hospital network in the country under the Metro Pacific Investments Corporation (MPIC), led by Mr. Manuel V. Pangilinan.
As member of the MPH network, PDH shares the same goal in providing medical excellence and compassionate care to all our patients.
PDH is a pillar of trust in healthcare for its neighboring community, driven by a deep passion for delivering patient-centered care across generations. With a comprehensive range of medical facilities—including a 94-bed inpatient facility, Emergency Room (ER), Intensive Care Unit (ICU), Neonatal Intensive Care Unit (NICU), Operating Room (OR), Delivery Room (DR), Laboratory, and Radiology, PDH is also accredited by the Department of Health (DOH), PhilHealth, and various Health Maintenance Organizations (HMOs), ensuring accessible healthcare and full compliance with government-regulated standards.
More than just a healthcare provider, PDH is a steadfast partner in the community’s journey to wellness.
Our Mission
To deliver “Alagang PDH”, our unique way of delivering high quality and compassionate care that is affordable, accessible, and patient-centered.
Our Vision
To be the leading healthcare provider, renowned for medical excellence, compassionate care, innovative healthcare services, and dedicated commitment to the well-being of the community.
Our Goal
To provide healthcare services that combine exceptional quality with affordability, making every experience at PDH truly worth it for our patients and their families.
Core Values
Excellence
Striving for the highest standards in healthcare delivery
Compassion
Delivering care with empathy and understanding
Innovation
Embracing modern medical advancements to enhance patient care
Integrity
Upholding transparency and ethical practices in all aspects of our operations
Teamwork
Collaborating effectively to achieve shared goals
Our Leadership Team
Guided by Purpose. Driven by Passion.

Our Barangay Partners
Building Healthier Communities, Together. At PDH, we believe that meaningful healthcare starts at the grassroots. Through our strong collaborations with barangay leaders and local communities, we bring essential health education, medical services, and support closer to the people. These partnerships are the cornerstone of our mission to make quality healthcare more accessible, inclusive, and impactful for all.
Barangay Don Bosco
We’re thrilled to partner with Barangay Don Bosco! Through our Memorandum of Agreement, we are committed to improving healthcare access for the community. Just present your Barangay ID or any valid ID to enjoy exclusive PDH benefits.
Barangay Sun Valley
PDH has partnered with Barangay Sun Valley to enhance healthcare access and wellness. Present proof of residence, like your Barangay ID, and enjoy exclusive health benefits at PDH.

TITLE: Alagang PDH Ako Stories:  A Mother’s Journey of Hope and Healing - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: For Jennifer Danao, January 2025 was a time of worry, uncertainty, and ultimately, gratitude. It was a challenging period in her life, but one marked by the dedication and expertise of the medical professionals who saved her six-year-old son, Lucio Tio.
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
Alagang PDH Ako Stories:
A Mother’s Journey of Hope and Healing
For Jennifer Danao, January 2025 was a time of worry, uncertainty, and ultimately, gratitude. It was a challenging period in her life, but one marked by the dedication and expertise of the medical professionals who saved her six-year-old son, Lucio Tio.
A Sudden Illness
Lucio was a lively and energetic child, always full of laughter and play. But when he suddenly developed a high fever, excruciating stomach pain, and relentless vomiting, Jennifer’s heart filled with fear. At first, she had hoped that it was just a passing illness, but as the pain in her son’s eyes deepened, she knew this was something far more serious. Panic turned into determination—she had to find the best possible care to save Lucio.
The Decision to Transfer to Parañaque Doctors Hospital
Among the hospitals, Jennifer chose Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH). Its Level 2 classification meant it had the necessary facilities and medical expertise for the procedure.
Additionally, it was only 30 minutes away and provided quality medical care at a more reasonable cost compared to her other options.
On January 21, 2025, Jennifer and Lucio arrived at PDH’s Emergency Room. The medical team immediately assessed Lucio’s condition and scheduled surgery on the same day.
Immediate Care and Expert Treatment
The PDH team of Professionals worked diligently to treat Lucio’s ruptured appendix. He was diagnosed with Generalized Peritonitis Secondary to Ruptured Appendicitis, a serious condition that, if left untreated, could have had life-threatening consequences.
Jennifer described the moments leading up to the surgery as overwhelming, but the PDH medical staff provided comfort and reassurance.
“The doctors, nurses, and staff were friendly, accommodating, and guided me through every step. From the time we arrived at the ER to the moment we were discharged, I felt assured that Lucio was in good hands,” she shared.
A Successful Recovery
The operation was successful. After five days of careful monitoring and continuous support from the PDH medical team, Lucio was discharged on January 26, 2025. Jennifer expressed deep gratitude, not only for the excellent medical treatment but also for the compassion and dedication shown by the hospital staff. “The doctors, nurses, and staff treated us greatly. The service was fast, and we never felt neglected. What I appreciated most about PDH was the genuine care they provided,” she said.
A Message of Gratitude
Now back home and fully recovered, Lucio is once again an active and happy child. Jennifer has no suggestions for improvement, only heartfelt appreciation for the team that saved her son’s life.
“To the entire PDH team, especially the doctors, thank you for everything. You gave my son another chance at life. To other parents and patients, know that at PDH, you and your loved ones will always receive the best care you deserve.” – Jennifer Danao
In times of medical emergencies, Parañaque Doctors Hospital remains a beacon of hope— providing expert care with compassion and dedication.

TITLE: Alagang PDH Ako Stories: More Than a Hospital: A Second Home Built on Trust and Care - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: At Parañaque Doctors Hospital, care goes beyond medicine. It’s about building trust, offering comfort, and creating a second home for families facing life’s toughest challenges. This is the story of Thelma and her family—a journey of hope, compassion, and unwavering support that defines what Alagang PDH truly means.
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
Alagang PDH Ako Stories: More Than a Hospital: A Second Home Built on Trust and Care
Finding Comfort and Compassion at Parañaque Doctors Hospital
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital, care goes beyond medicine. It’s about building trust, offering comfort, and creating a second home for families facing life’s toughest challenges. This is the story of Thelma and her family—a journey of hope, compassion, and unwavering support that defines what Alagang PDH truly means.
“What stands out most to us is the consistent warmth, attentiveness, and genuine care we receive from the PDH team—proof that compassion is at the heart of every visit.”
— Rene, Thelma’s Husband, PDH Patient Companion
A Journey That Led Home
For many families, the journey through illness can be a heavy burden. But for one couple—empty nesters who’ve built a life filled with love, laughter, and resilience—their story is also one of deep gratitude.
Since 2022, PDH has become their second home. Thelma, the patient, was diagnosed with myelodysplastic syndrome (MDS) in 2021. Guided by their son, a doctor, they sought care that would not only provide proper treatment but also offer comfort, familiarity, and peace of mind.
More Than Medicine: The Power of Connection
It wasn’t a difficult choice. Their trusted family doctor, Dr. Rico Tee, is a regular consultant at PDH. So did other familiar faces—Thelma’s sister-in-law, a childhood friend, and even their own son who occasionally performs procedures at the hospital. “Everything just felt like it fit,” her husband shared. “Why go far when good care is close by?”
From the start, the PDH team—from the doctors and nurses to the staff at the admitting office and blood bank—has made them feel seen, heard, and genuinely cared for. “They’re knowledgeable, accommodating, and they treat us like family,” he said, recalling names of staff they now know by heart. Even the hospital guards and maintenance crew have become part of their routine visits, offering familiar greetings that feel like home.
Defining Alaga in the Smallest Ways
What stands out most to them is the consistent warmth, attentiveness, and genuine care they receive from the entire PDH team—proof that compassion is at the heart of every visit.
When asked what keeps them coming back, he smiles and says, “It’s not just the treatment. It’s trust. It’s connection. It’s feeling like we’re in good hands.”
This is what Alagang PDH means to them—more than care, it’s family.
At PDH, the true healing comes not just from medicine, but from the heartfelt compassion and unwavering support that embraces every patient like family. Here, hope is nurtured, and no one walks their journey alone.
#AlagangPDHAko

TITLE: Alagang PDH Barangay Partnership - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we believe that quality healthcare should be accessible, affordable, and tailored to the needs of our community. Through the Alagang PDH Barangay Partnership, we are strengthening our commitment to serving you better by collaborating with local barangays to bring exclusive healthcare benefits right to your neighborhood!
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
Alagang PDH Barangay Partnership
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we believe that quality healthcare should be accessible, affordable, and tailored to the needs of our community. Through the Alagang PDH Barangay Partnership, we are strengthening our commitment to serving you better by collaborating with local barangays to bring exclusive healthcare benefits right to your neighborhood!
Exclusive Discounts for Our Ka-Barangay!
Your health is our priority! As part of this special partnership, residents of participating barangays can enjoy 5% discounts on Laboratory and Room Fees—ensuring quality care at a more affordable price. No hassle, no fuss—just trusted medical services from your healthcare partner, PDH.
How to Avail?
It’s easy! Simply present your Barangay ID along with any valid government-issued ID that shows your name and address at PDH.
Reminders:
- Senior Citizens and PWDs—only the highest discount applies.
- Discount is valid for cash payments only.
Partnering with Barangays for a Healthier Tomorrow
The Alagang PDH Barangay Partnership is more than just discounts. It is a commitment to building a healthier and stronger community. Through our partnerships, we will also conduct medical missions, health education programs, and wellness initiatives to ensure that every resident has access to essential healthcare services.
Barangays Under the PDH Partnership:
- Barangay Don Bosco
- Barangay Sun Valley
- Barangay Marcelo Green
- Barangay San Antonio
- Barangay Upper Bicutan
- Barangay Napindan
- Latest Addition: Barangay Moonwalk
Dahil sa PDH, Alagang PDH Ako!
Stay tuned for more updates as we continue to expand our barangay partnerships and bring healthcare closer to you. Because at PDH, we don’t just care—we make sure you feel it!

Author: Bryan Gutierrez
Alagang PDH Ako Stories: More Than a Hospital: A Second Home Built on Trust and Care
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital, care goes beyond medicine. It’s about building trust, offering comfort, and creating a second home for families facing life’s toughest challenges. This is the story of Thelma and her family—a journey of hope, compassion, and unwavering support that defines what Alagang PDH truly means.
Neuro Talk Kick-Off: Empowering Barangay Napindan with Life-Saving Knowledge
On March 27, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) took an important step toward community health empowerment with the Kick-Off: Neuro Talk at Barangay Napindan. This engaging and informative event shed light on one of the most critical health concerns today—stroke awareness and prevention—proving that learning about health can be both educational and enjoyable!
IM Family Health Talk Kick-Off: Building Stronger Families, One Health Talk at a Time
On March 24, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) proudly launched its IM Family Health Talk in Barangay Upper Bicutan, led by Dr. Keith Darryl Deo Grafil. This initiative was designed to empower families with essential health knowledge while ensuring that learning remains interactive, engaging, and fun!
Keeping Hearts Healthy: PDH’s Cardiovascular Health Talk Brings Awareness to the Community
On March 20, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) took a proactive step toward heart health awareness by hosting an insightful Cardiovascular Health Talk, led by Dr. Paul Quetua, Internal Medicine Specialist. The event aimed to equip the community with lifesaving knowledge on preventing cardiovascular diseases, debunking common heart health myths, and promoting a heart-healthy lifestyle.
Parenting 101: Because Every Child Deserves the Best Start!
On March 17, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) hosted an insightful Parenting 101 Talk, led by Dr. Maria Theresa De Jesus, Pediatrician, as part of our Family Health Awareness series.
PDH Buntis Day 2025: Nurturing Mothers, Strengthening Communities
PDH News On March 10, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) celebrated Buntis Day, a special gathering dedicated to expectant mothers and their journey to motherhood. This event, filled with education, support, and heartfelt moments, reaffirmed PDH’s commitment to providing expert maternal care with a compassionate touch. With the guidance of our esteemed OB-GYN specialists—Dr. […]
Alagang PDH Ako Stories:
A Mother’s Journey of Hope and Healing
For Jennifer Danao, January 2025 was a time of worry, uncertainty, and ultimately, gratitude. It was a challenging period in her life, but one marked by the dedication and expertise of the medical professionals who saved her six-year-old son, Lucio Tio.
Alagang PDH Barangay Partnership
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we believe that quality healthcare should be accessible, affordable, and tailored to the needs of our community. Through the Alagang PDH Barangay Partnership, we are strengthening our commitment to serving you better by collaborating with local barangays to bring exclusive healthcare benefits right to your neighborhood!

On March 26, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) hosted a Bloodletting Drive, and it was
Importance of Regular Check-ups
Have You Been Hearing About Regular Check-Ups? Here’s Why They’re Essential for Your Health
You’ve probably heard doctors, health talks, events, or even your friends and family emphasize the importance of regular check-ups—but do you really know why they matter? Many people only visit a doctor when they feel sick, but did you know that preventive healthcare is just as crucial?
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we advocate for regular medical check-ups to ensure early detection, better management of health conditions, and overall well-being.

TITLE: Accounting Manager - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: PDH Careers Qualification: Bachelor’s Degree in Accounting, Finance, or a related field (e.g., Business Administration).CPA (Certified Public Accountant) designation is highly preferred or required.Minimum of 5–7 years of experience in accounting or finance, with at least 2–3 years in a supervisory or managerial role. Proven experience in preparing financial reports, budgeting, and managing audits. Experience […]
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH Careers
Accounting Manager
Qualification:
Bachelor’s Degree in Accounting, Finance, or a related field (e.g., Business Administration).
CPA (Certified Public Accountant) designation is highly preferred or required.
Minimum of 5–7 years of experience in accounting or finance, with at least 2–3 years in a supervisory or managerial role. Proven experience in preparing financial reports, budgeting, and managing audits. Experience in managing a team and coordinating with various departments.
Job Description
- Handles the company General Ledger
- In charge in the preparation of Financial Statements (Balance Sheet, Income Statement and its corresponding attachments)
- Assist in the preparation of Reports
- Review and audit journal entries in FMS
- Review withholding tax expanded schedules
- Prepare monthly and quarterly ITR
- Supervise the Accounts Payable section
- Review vouchers and payments to supplier (Trade and Non-Trade)
- Monitor and record monthly accrual of expenses
- Monitor and record monthly amortization of prepayments
- Record manually cost of sales
- Process period and post-closing entries
- Create new/additional income and balance sheet account
- Prepare weekly revenue and admission monitoring
- Primary responsible in year-end audit by SGV
- Responsible in preparing cleared consolidation reporting package at year end
- Assists in the preparation of budget
- Develops, implements and maintains general accounting systems and controls, supervises and participates in the review and analysis of accounting records.
- Encoding of monthly e-sales report
- Prepare inventory list at year end for BIR Submission
- Review transactions requested for cancellation/voiding
- Review and record inventory adjustments
- Handles any task that may be assigned from time to time.
Apply for this Position

################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH Careers
Accounting Staff
Qualification:
Bachelor of Science in Accountancy or Business-Related course and Should have 1-3 years’ experience as Accounting Assistant.
Job Description
Assist in the preparation of tax returns (e.g., VAT, income tax, withholding tax).
File BIR forms and other government compliance reports, either manually or via eFPS/eBIR.
Gather and organize supporting documents such as invoices, official receipts, and BIR Forms 2307.
Reconcile tax reports with accounting records (e.g., sales vs. VAT returns).
Assist in reconciling Expanded Withholding Tax (EWT) and final tax certificates (e.g., Forms 2307 and 2316).

################################################################################

Skip to content
Accounts Receiveable
Job Description
- Maintain customer accounts: Maintain records of customer accounts and ensure accurate records.
- Generate invoices: Create invoices and account statements.
- Collect payments: Collect payments from customers and post receipts.
- Reconcile accounts: Reconcile accounts receivable ledgers with general ledgers.
- Resolve discrepancies: Resolve discrepancies between customer payments and invoices.
- Produce reports: Create monthly financial reports.
- Investigate inquiries: Investigate and resolve irregularities or inquiries.
- Support audits: Support internal and external audits.
- Apply credit practices: Apply fair credit practices.

ECHO Tech
Qualification:
Graduate of Radiologic Technology with PRC License. Preferably with Echo Training.
Job Description
- Preparing patients for echocardiographic procedures.
- Operating and maintaining ultrasound or echocardiography equipment.
- Capturing high-quality images of the heart and surrounding structures.
- Providing preliminary analysis and findings to physicians.
- Ensuring patient comfort and safety during procedures.
- Keeping accurate and detailed patient records.
- Collaborating with cardiologists and other healthcare professionals.
- Assisting physicians during complex echocardiographic examinations.

PDH Careers
Financial Analyst
Qualification:
Bachelor of Science in Accountancy or Business-Related course and Should have 1-3 years’ experience as Accounting Assistant.
Job Description
- Provide timely, relevant and accurate reporting and analysis of the results of the hospital and departmental performance against historical, budgeted, forecasted and strategic planning results to facilitate decision-making towards the achievement of the budget and strategic plan.
- Maintain and develop various financial models and standard templates for use by all of Finance during the planning process, ensuring quality, accuracy and focused analytic review.
- Prepare OPEX and other Income analysis with comparison (per department and for hospital as a whole).
- Analyze data to ensure that proper accounting procedure have been followed.
- Responsible in preparation of Quarterly reports to SEC and MPIC.
- Handle the reports needed by Philippine Statistics office regarding Financial Statements.
- Responsible for performing special projects assigned by the management.
- Responsible in completing the Budget report, and send such to Management.
- Perform task that may be needed during the yearly audit.
- Primarily responsible in preparation of Draft Financial Statement for SGV.
- Primarily responsible in completing all the requirements needed by SEC and SGV during and after the Audit of Financial Statements.
- Handles any task that may be assigned from time to time.

PDH Careers
HMO Staff
Qualification:
Must be a graduate of any 4 year course and computer literate.
Job Description
- Must provide efficient customer service to patients.
- Report daily census of processed LOA of procedures and consultations.
- Also assist HMO Liaison officers on any concerns, both operational and administrative.
- Assist in verifying patient’s coverages and HMO details.
- Assist patients at the window for any inquiries, both consultation and procedure.
- Verify patient’s member status.
- Call up HMO providers for outpatient approvals/ inquiries.
- Update doctors list on-leave for the day or on vacation.
- Answer telephone inquiries regarding any concerns related to HMO.
- Verify available doctors per schedule at information department.
- Check availability of forms per HMO provider and coordinate with them for request.
- Must collaborate and coordinate with other departments to be able to process LOA requests.
- To do other related duties or activities which may be assigned by his/her superior from time to time.

PDH Careers
Marketing Officer
Qualifications:
Organizational skills.
Customer service skills.
Technology and software skills.
College Degree Holder.
With Experience in Supervisory.
Basic knowledge in MS excel, powerpoint and fast learner.
Experience in healthcare industry is a plus.
Job Description
The primary role is to promote hospital services to patients and customers, aiming to establish a recognized brand, enhance the hospital’s reputation, and increase company revenue. The position focuses on:
- Attracting attention and generating interest in the hospital’s services.
- Creating and maintaining a strong brand image.
- Implementing marketing strategies to engage, inform, and retain patients.
- Ensure that daily operations, company procedures, and customer service standards are consistently followed.
- Complete sales and marketing administrative tasks, including running marketing reports, conducting research, and maintaining databases.
- Develop promotional materials for hospital services, health packages, and special offers.
- Manage branding and public relations, including promotional strategies and product development.
- Organize health-related events, sponsorships, and community outreach programs.
- Provide logistical support for events, seminars, and trade shows.
- Assist in writing and editing sales and marketing materials, including articles, presentations, and promotional content.
- Maintain project tracking spreadsheets and business marketing calendars, ensuring all stakeholders execute the Marketing Calendar effectively.
- Act as a liaison between the hospital and advertising agencies, print suppliers, freelance talent, and marketing service providers.
- Prepare event invitations, agendas, track payments or refunds, and registration forms for hospital-led events.
- Provide personal and administrative support to directors, including coordinating travel, scheduling appointments, and preparing agendas.
- Manage marketing and media event budgets, ensuring smooth execution of trade shows and seminars.
- Regularly coordinate and collaborate with other departments to ensure successful execution of marketing initiatives.
- Perform other duties assigned by management as needed.

Skip to content
PDH Careers
Phlebotomist
Qualification:
BS – Medical Technologist with PRC License
Job Description
- Receives laboratory requests and verifies that all necessary information, particularly the requested laboratory examinations, is correctly indicated.
- Ensures that all laboratory requests received are properly charged.
- Responsible for the extraction of blood samples at the ODP, ER and In Patients using established laboratory techniques.
- Ensures proper labeling of all specimen received and makes separate requisitions for laboratory requests
with multiple procedures for processing to the different sections of the laboratory.
- Assists the Blood Blank staff in bleeding of blood donors.
- Responsible for the monitoring and calibration of glucometers found in each station.
- Responsible for the maintenance and adequacy of extraction materials at the phlebotomy area.
- Performs all other related works that will be assigned to him in the exigency of the service.
- Release results to OUT patients and IN patients.
- To do other duties or activities which my assigned by his/her superior from time to time.

Skip to content
PDH Careers
Respiratory Therapist
Qualification:
Must be a graduate of RT and has License. Fresh Graduates are welcome to Apply.
Job Description
Performing therapeutic respiratory procedures (aerosol therapy).
Carrying out diagnostic pulmonary procedures (pulmonary function test, peak flow meter).
Teaching patients how to use incentive spirometers and metered-dose inhalers.
Performing bronchopulmonary drainage.
Assisting with breathing exercises.
Applying universal precautions and using personal protective equipment.
Coordinating with other departments regarding patient care.
Providing brief education to patients and families about their respiratory healthcare needs.
Conducting daily rounds on patients receiving nebulization treatment and ensuring proper machine function.
Acting in the capacity of a superior and performing assigned duties and responsibilities in their absence.
Conducting supported pulmonary procedures.
Ensuring proper documentation in patients’ charts.
Encoding and releasing patients’ results.
Organizing logbooks with patients’ records.
Maintaining stocks and supplies needed for day-to-day operations.
Properly endorsing work to incoming respiratory therapy staff and the pulmonary nurse on duty.
Performing other duties or activities as assigned by a superior from time to time.

Essential knowledge and skills of the principles, methods, materials, equipment and techniques of quality […]
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH Careers
Systems, Processes & Technology Division Head
Qualification:
1. Bachelor’s degree from an accredited college or university, preferably PRC registered medical professional with updated license.
2. At least 5 years work experience in a hospital setting, with 3 years holding a supervisory or managerial position.
3. Essential knowledge and skills of the principles, methods, materials, equipment and techniques of quality management.
4. Excellent organizational, time management and coordinating skills.
5. Excellent ability to design, develop, implement, and evaluate training plans, curricula, and including QA studies and methodology.
6. Sound teaching and facilitation skills.
7. Computer literacy, with knowledge on current software.
8. Proficient in the use of Microsoft Office applications, email and Internet.
Job Description
- Ensures conformance with DOH, PHIC, ISO, compliance to Data Privacy Act, Safety Standards and Quality Management System standards by ensuring 100% compilation of enumerated sources of Quality Improvement initiatives.
- Assures systematic and ongoing review of departmental processes, procedures, activities, equipment performance, and contracted services to increase the department’s efficiency, effectiveness, and quality, with emphasis on the recording of documents.
- Supervises a program of internal audits against defined quality performance measures and ensure that effective corrective and follow up actions are taken, ensures maintenance of a safe and healthy working environment and compliance of safe working practices, prepares compliance reports to present and discuss with Quality Management, manages coordination.
- Initiates, attends, or facilitates policy review activities.
- Identifies areas and projects where there is the potential for improvement of quality to benefit of the service provided by the hospital. Participates in meetings, decision making and policy implementation as directed. Collaborates with Unit/Section/Division Heads in educating and training staff in quality principles and practice.
- Cost effectiveness and reduction of operating expense in utilizing resources with the approved budget for the year in operational and administrative functions.
- Familiarizes relevant statutory requirements, clinical governance, risk management and quality audit policies and procedures of the hospital and the system of accreditation in use.
- Responsible for monitoring of the enumerated sources of Quality Improvement initiatives

URL: https://pdh.com.ph/careers/staff-nurse/
TITLE: Staff Nurse - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: PDH Careers Qualification: We are seeking a compassionate and dedicated Staff Nurse to provide high-quality patient care in a fast-paced healthcare environment. Responsibilities include monitoring patient health, administering medications, coordinating with the healthcare team, and ensuring a safe, supportive atmosphere. The ideal candidate is empathetic, detail-oriented, and committed to patient well-being and professional excellence. Job […]
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH Careers
Staff Nurse
Qualification:
We are seeking a compassionate and dedicated Staff Nurse to provide high-quality patient care in a fast-paced healthcare environment. Responsibilities include monitoring patient health, administering medications, coordinating with the healthcare team, and ensuring a safe, supportive atmosphere. The ideal candidate is empathetic, detail-oriented, and committed to patient well-being and professional excellence.
Job Description
1. Gives direct nursing care.
2. Identifies nursing needs of patients.
3. Plans, gives and evaluate nursing care.
4. Admits patient.
5. Performs such nursing activities such as bathing and oral hygiene.
6. Assists patient in moving, maintaining proper alignment of patient’s body and utilizing good body mechanic.
7. Takes and record vital sign.
8. Observes medical sepsis.
9. Observes signs and symptoms, institutes remedial action when appropriate and record these in her chart.
10. Report observations to the physician.
11. Observes and responds to patients’ emotional spiritual need.
12. Acts as liaison between patients and hospital personnel.
13. Interprets to the patient and his family their roles in promoting successful therapy and rehabilitation.
14. Prepares patients for and assists physicians with diagnostics and therapeutic procedure.
15. Carries out doctor’s orders.
16. Assist physicians when examining patients.
17. Performs irrigations, catheterizations and gives enema.
18. Administers and charts medication and patients’ reaction to medications.
19. Instruct patients and families.
20. Sees to it that equipment receives proper care maintenance.
21. Discharge patients.
22. Performs post- mortem care.
23. Teaches and direct new professional nursing personnel.
24. Acts as head nurse when so delegated.
25. Receive and endorses ward equipment and supplies.
26. Interpret hospital policies and procedures.
27. Gives health teaching.
28. Maintains good relationship with other hospital personnel.
29. Charts accurately medications given.
30. Attends meetings and conferences.
31. Is subject to cross training based on performance evaluation, and once assigned in the special area (OR-DR, NICU, ICU, PICU and ER) they shall observe whatever responsibilities, policies and procedures to be carried out for patient care that is imposed on that certain department under PCS.
32. To do other duties or activities that may be assigned by your superior from time to time.
Apply for this Position

Importance of Regular Check-ups
Have You Been Hearing About Regular Check-Ups? Here’s Why They’re Essential for Your Health
You’ve probably heard doctors, health talks, events, or even your friends and family emphasize the importance of regular check-ups—but do you really know why they matter? Many people only visit a doctor when they feel sick, but did you know that preventive healthcare is just as crucial?
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we advocate for regular medical check-ups to ensure early detection, better management of health conditions, and overall well-being.
5 Tips for Risk Reduction and Early Detection for Cancer
February marks Cancer Awareness Month, a time to shed light on the importance of early detection, prevention, and treatment. At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we are committed to providing top-notch medical care and diagnostic services to help our community stay informed and proactive about their health.

Skip to content
PDH News
Every Drop Counts: PDH Bloodletting Drive Saves Lives
On March 26, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) hosted a Bloodletting Drive, and it was nothing short of amazing! With the selfless generosity of our donors, we didn’t just collect blood—we gave hope, second chances, and the gift of life to those in need.
A Simple Act, A Life-Saving Impact
Every drop of blood donated has the power to:
- Save lives – Especially for patients in emergency surgeries, cancer treatments, and those battling blood disorders.
- Support the community – Providing hospitals with a steady blood supply for those whoneed it most.
- Boost health – Donors also benefit, as regular blood donation encourages better blood circulation and reduces certain health risks.
Heroes Among Us
A huge congratulations and heartfelt thank you to all our blood donors—you are the true heroes of this event! Your kindness and compassion remind us that even the simplest gestures can have the biggest impact.
Dahil sa Alagang PDH Ako, We Don’t Just Care—We Share Life!
At PDH, we are committed to making healthcare accessible, impactful, and life-changing. This blood drive is just one of the many ways we give back to the community and help save lives.
Be a hero. Be a donor. And share the Alagang PDH. See you at the next one?

TITLE: IM Family Health Talk Kick-Off: Building Stronger Families, One Health Talk at a Time - Parañaque Doctors Hospital

Skip to content
PDH News
IM Family Health Talk Kick-Off: Building Stronger Families, One Health Talk at a Time
On March 24, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) proudly launched its IM Family Health Talk in Barangay Upper Bicutan, led by Dr. Keith Darryl Deo Grafil. This initiative was designed to empower families with essential health knowledge while ensuring that learning remains interactive, engaging, and fun!
With summer just around the corner, staying informed about preventive healthcare measures and debunking common medical myths is more important than ever. Residents gained valuable insights into disease prevention, with a strong focus on dengue awareness and healthy lifestyle habits—helping them make well-informed choices for themselves and their families.
But who says learning about health has to be all serious? The event was packed with energy, excitement, and fun! Through interactive discussions, engaging games, and exciting prizes, participants not only learned vital health information but also enjoyed every moment of it. The joy and laughter shared throughout the day proved that staying healthy can be both educational and entertaining!
PDH extends its deepest gratitude to Honorable Francis Sunga (Barangay Chairman), Barangay Secretary Angie Libutan, Barangay Admin Rima B. Trapal, and the entire Barangay Upper Bicutan team for their unwavering support. Your enthusiasm and dedication to community health made this event a resounding success.
At PDH, we believe that knowledge is the foundation of a healthier community—and this is just the beginning! With continued partnerships like this, we look forward to bringing healthcare closer to more communities, ensuring that quality health education is accessible to all.

You’ve probably heard doctors, health talks, events, or even your friends and family emphasize the importance of regular check-ups—but do you really know why they matter? Many people only visit a doctor when they feel sick, but did you know that preventive healthcare is just as crucial?
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we advocate for regular medical check-ups to ensure early detection, better management of health conditions, and overall well-being.
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
Importance of Regular Check-ups
Have You Been Hearing About Regular Check-Ups? Here’s Why They’re Essential for Your Health
You’ve probably heard doctors, health talks, events, or even your friends and family emphasize the importance of regular check-ups—but do you really know why they matter? Many people only visit a doctor when they feel sick, but did you know that preventive healthcare is just as crucial?
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH), we advocate for regular medical check-ups to ensure early detection, better management of health conditions, and overall well-being.
5 Reasons for you to do a Regular Check up
- Early Detection Saves Lives Regular check-ups help detect health issues before they become severe. Conditions like hypertension, diabetes, and even cancer can develop silently, showing symptoms only in later stages.
At PDH, we offer complete laboratory tests, imaging services, and executive check-up packages tailored to your needs.
- Keeps Chronic Diseases Under Control If you already have a medical condition, routine check-ups ensure that your treatment plan is working effectively. Regular monitoring helps in adjusting medications and lifestyle recommendations.
Our Internal Medicine Specialists at PDH provide expert guidance on managing chronic conditions.
- Improves Overall Wellness Regular doctor visits are not just about illness; they also focus on overall wellness, including mental health, nutrition, and lifestyle improvement.
PDH offer personalized consultations to help you achieve a healthier lifestyle.
- Saves Money in the Long Run Preventive care is often more affordable than treating advanced diseases. Investing in regular check-ups can reduce hospitalizations and expensive treatments.
PDH offers cost-effective health screening packages to help you stay ahead of potential health risks.
- Peace of Mind Knowing that you are in good health or catching potential concerns early provides a sense of security and reduces health-related anxiety.
PDH’s facilities and experienced medical professionals ensure accurate and reliable health assessments.
Why Choose Parañaque Doctors Hospital for Your Regular Check-Ups? At PDH, we believe that prevention is better than cure. Our reliable facilities, experienced doctors, and patient-centered care approach make us the best choice for your routine health check-ups.
Your health is your greatest asset—take action today! For appointments and inquiries, call 8776-0644 to 46, local 2 or visit us at info@pdh.com.ph.

TITLE: Keeping Hearts Healthy: PDH’s Cardiovascular Health Talk Brings Awareness to the Community - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: On March 20, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) took a proactive step toward heart health awareness by hosting an insightful Cardiovascular Health Talk, led by Dr. Paul Quetua, Internal Medicine Specialist. The event aimed to equip the community with lifesaving knowledge on preventing cardiovascular diseases, debunking common heart health myths, and promoting a heart-healthy lifestyle.
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
Keeping Hearts Healthy: PDH’s Cardiovascular Health Talk Brings Awareness to the Community
On March 20, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) took a proactive step toward heart health awareness by hosting an insightful Cardiovascular Health Talk, led by Dr. Paul Quetua, Internal Medicine Specialist. The event aimed to equip the community with lifesaving knowledge on preventing cardiovascular diseases, debunking common heart health myths, and promoting a heart-healthy lifestyle.
A United Effort for Healthier Hearts
This event wouldn’t have been a success without the unwavering support of Barangay Marcelo Green and Barangay Don Bosco, whose leaders showed their dedication to promoting health and wellness.
Barangay Marcelo Green Shows Their Heart!
A huge shoutout to Barangay Captain Ian V. Aguilar, Ma’am Myrna Aguilar, Health Committee Head Sir Menandro “Popoy” Molina, and Executive Secretary Ma’am Nicole Lirazan for their active participation. Their presence underscored their commitment to ensuring their community understands the importance of heart disease prevention.
Barangay Don Bosco Cares for Their Hearts!
We also extend our gratitude to Barangay Captain Mar Jimenez and Barangay Secretary Ma’am Milagros “Miles” Cruz, who, alongside their community members, embraced this opportunity to learn more about heart health. With the right knowledge, they are now better equipped to make healthier choices for themselves and their families.
More Than Just a Health Talk—It Was a Celebration!
At PDH, we believe that learning should be fun and engaging! That’s why we didn’t just share important heart health tips—we also made sure our participants had a great time!
Seeing the smiles and energy of our attendees proved that health education can be both meaningful and enjoyable!
Prevention Starts with Awareness
Cardiovascular diseases remain one of the leading causes of illness worldwide—but with the right knowledge and lifestyle choices, they are largely preventable. At PDH, we stand by the belief that prevention is the best cure—and it starts with empowering communities through accessible health education.
Thank you to everyone who joined us! Together, let’s keep our hearts strong and our communities healthier.
Kalingang ramdam, serbisyong matibay — ‘yan ang PDH!

TITLE: Neuro Talk Kick-Off: Empowering Barangay Napindan with Life-Saving Knowledge - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: On March 27, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) took an important step toward community health empowerment with the Kick-Off: Neuro Talk at Barangay Napindan. This engaging and informative event shed light on one of the most critical health concerns today—stroke awareness and prevention—proving that learning about health can be both educational and enjoyable!
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
Neuro Talk Kick-Off: Empowering Barangay Napindan with Life-Saving Knowledge
On March 27, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) took an important step toward community health empowerment with the Kick-Off: Neuro Talk at Barangay Napindan. This engaging and informative event shed light on one of the most critical health concerns today—stroke awareness and prevention—proving that learning about health can be both educational and enjoyable!
Led by PDH’s expert Neurologist, Dr. Aubrey Punzalan, residents gained valuable insights into stroke warning signs, prevention strategies, and the importance of early detection. Stroke remains a leading cause of disability and death worldwide, but with the right knowledge and proactive lifestyle changes, its risks can be significantly reduced.
More than just a talk, the event was a true community celebration! Interactive games, exciting prizes, and special giveaways made the learning experience fun and engaging, ensuring that every participant left not only informed but also with smiles on their faces.This successful initiative would not have been possible without the warm support of Barangay Chairman Engr. Gerome Michael S. San Pedro, Barangay Secretary Arlene Ricahuerta, and the entire Barangay Napindan Council and, of course, its residents. Their dedication to community well-being played a key role in making this event impactful.
At PDH, we believe that prevention starts with awareness. Through partnerships like this, we remain committed to bringing quality healthcare, expert knowledge, and compassionate service straight to the heart of our communities.
‘Yan ang Alagang PDH Ako!

Our Services
Laboratory Services
Our laboratory is fully equipped to conduct a wide range of diagnostic tests, including hematology, clinical microscopy, clinical chemistry, microbiology, immunology, and serology. The laboratory staff ensures quick and accurate results, which are essential for effective treatment planning. With a focus on precision and patient safety, PDH’s laboratory services meet the highest standards in the healthcare industry.

TITLE: Parenting 101: Because Every Child Deserves the Best Start! - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: On March 17, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) hosted an insightful Parenting 101 Talk, led by Dr. Maria Theresa De Jesus, Pediatrician, as part of our Family Health Awareness series.
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
Parenting 101: Because Every Child Deserves the Best Start!
On March 17, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) hosted an insightful Parenting 101 Talk, led by Dr. Maria Theresa De Jesus, Pediatrician, as part of our Family Health Awareness series.
This event was designed to equip parents and caregivers with essential knowledge on raising happy, healthy children while making the learning process engaging and interactive.
Empowering Parents, Nurturing Families
Parenting is a lifelong journey filled with love, challenges, and learning. During the talk, Dr. De Jesus guided attendees through key topics, including:
- Common Parenting Myths – Separating fact from fiction in child-rearing.
- Child Nutrition and Health – What every parent should know about feeding and caring for their little ones.
- Preventive Healthcare – The importance of regular check-ups, vaccinations, and early health screenings.
- Positive Parenting Techniques – How to foster a loving and supportive environment at home.
More Than Just a Talk—A Fun and Interactive Experience!
Because learning should be fun, the event also featured:
- Exciting games that reinforced key parenting lessons.
- Raffle prizes and giveaways to make the experience even more special.
- A supportive community of parents sharing experiences and insights.
The smiles and laughter from our attendees proved that parenting education doesn’t have to be overwhelming—it can be enjoyable, too!
Alagang PDH Ako: Supporting Families Every Step of the Way
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital, we believe that strong families build strong communities. Through events like Parenting 101, we continue to empower parents with the knowledge and tools they need to give their children the best start in life.
A huge thank you to all the parents and caregivers who participated—we admire your dedication to your child’s well-being! Stay tuned for more family-centered health talks and programs at PDH.
Be the parent your child needs. Choose Alagang PDH Ako!

URL: https://pdh.com.ph/pdh-buntis-day-2025-nurturing-mothers-strengthening-communities/
TITLE: PDH Buntis Day 2025: Nurturing Mothers, Strengthening Communities - Parañaque Doctors Hospital
DESCRIPTION: PDH News On March 10, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) celebrated Buntis Day, a special gathering dedicated to expectant mothers and their journey to motherhood. This event, filled with education, support, and heartfelt moments, reaffirmed PDH’s commitment to providing expert maternal care with a compassionate touch.   With the guidance of our esteemed OB-GYN specialists—Dr. […]
################################################################################

Skip to content
PDH News
PDH Buntis Day 2025: Nurturing Mothers, Strengthening Communities
On March 10, 2025, Parañaque Doctors Hospital (PDH) celebrated Buntis Day, a special gathering dedicated to expectant mothers and their journey to motherhood. This event, filled with education, support, and heartfelt moments, reaffirmed PDH’s commitment to providing expert maternal care with a compassionate touch.
With the guidance of our esteemed OB-GYN specialists—Dr. Ada Angela Cabrera, Dr. Emilyn Areglado, and Dr. Anna Lyn Magbuhos—attendees gained essential knowledge on prenatal care, safe labor and delivery, postpartum wellness, breastfeeding, and informed family planning choices. These discussions empowered soon-to-be moms with the confidence and information they need for a healthy and safe pregnancy.
Beyond medical insights, the event was made even more memorable with the Ms. Buntis Day Pageant, where mothers proudly shared their unique pregnancy experiences. Their inspiring stories of strength, resilience, and love resonated deeply with everyone present, making the celebration even more meaningful.
This remarkable day would not have been possible without the unwavering support of Barangay Don Bosco and Barangay Marcelo Green. We extend our gratitude to Barangay Chairman Mar Jimenez and Barangay Chairman Ian V. Aguilar for their dedication to the well-being of their communities. Their partnership with PDH highlights the power of collaboration in making quality healthcare more accessible to all.
At PDH, we believe that motherhood is a journey best taken with care, guidance, and community support. Thank you to everyone who joined us in celebrating this special day.
Dito lang ‘yan sa PDH—where every mother and baby receive the best care possible!

DATA PRIVACY NOTICE
Your Privacy Matters
At Parañaque Doctors Hospital, we value your privacy and are committed to protecting your personal information in accordance with the Data Privacy Act of 2012 (Republic Act No. 10173), its Implementing Rules and Regulations, and other applicable laws and regulations.
Personal Information We Collect
While providing healthcare services, we may collect and process the following personal information:
- Personal and demographic information (e.g., name, address, date of birth, sex, civil status, nationality)
- Contact information (e.g., mobile number and email address)
- Government-issued identification numbers
- PhilHealth, HMO, and insurance information
- Medical history and health records
- Laboratory and diagnostic results
- Prescriptions and treatment records
- Billing and payment information
- Employment information (for employees and applicants)
- CCTV recordings and visitor logs
- System-generated information such as user accounts, access logs, and IP addresses when using our online services
Purpose of Processing
Your personal information is processed to:
- Provide safe, efficient, and quality healthcare services.
- Register and identify patients.
- Diagnose, treat, and monitor medical conditions.
- Schedule appointments and manage admissions and discharges.
- Process laboratory, pharmacy, radiology, and other ancillary services.
- Process billing, insurance, PhilHealth, and HMO claims.
- Communicate important healthcare and administrative information.
- Maintain medical records and comply with legal, regulatory, and accreditation requirements.
- Protect patients, employees, visitors, and hospital property through security measures.
- Improve hospital services, quality management, and operational efficiency.
- Perform other legitimate hospital functions permitted by law.
PDH processes personal data in accordance with the principles of transparency, legitimate purpose, and proportionality.
Legal Basis for Processing
We process your personal information based on one or more of the following lawful bases:
- Your consent, where required by law.
- Performance of a contract for the provision of healthcare services.
- Protection of your life and health.
- Compliance with legal and regulatory obligations.
- The hospital’s legitimate interests, provided these do not override your rights and freedoms.
Data Sharing
Your personal information may be shared only when necessary and authorized with:
- Physicians and healthcare professionals involved in your care.
- Authorized hospital personnel.
- PhilHealth, HMOs, insurance companies, and other healthcare payors.
- Government agencies as required by law.
- Accredited service providers acting on behalf of the hospital under appropriate confidentiality and data protection agreements.
- Other parties with your consent or as otherwise permitted by applicable law.
How We Protect Your Personal Data
PDH implements organizational, physical, and technical safeguards to protect the confidentiality, integrity, and availability of personal data.
These measures include, among others:
- Appointment of a Data Protection Officer;
- Data privacy and security policies and procedures;
- Privacy and data protection training;
- Role-based access and authorization controls;
- Secure storage of physical records;
- Access controls for restricted areas;
- User authentication and account management;
- Security controls for hospital information systems and networks;
- Periodic review of access privileges;
- Incident and personal data breach response procedures;
- Secure transmission and sharing of personal data; and
- Regular monitoring, assessment, and improvement of privacy and security controls.
The DPA and its IRR require reasonable and appropriate organizational, physical, and technical security measures for the protection of personal data.
Risk
Privacy risk refers to the possibility that personal data may be accidentally or unlawfully accessed, disclosed, altered, lost, destroyed, or otherwise processed without authorization.
PDH implements appropriate safeguards to minimize, monitor, and manage risks associated with the processing of personal data, particularly information relating to patients and their health.
Storage
Physical records containing personal data are stored in secured areas within the Hospital and are accessible only to authorized personnel.
Electronic records are stored in authorized hospital information systems, databases, servers, and other approved information technology environments protected by appropriate organizational, physical, and technical safeguards.
Where personal data is processed or stored by authorized third-party service providers, PDH implements appropriate contractual, security, and privacy measures consistent with applicable requirements.
Data Retention and Disposal
Parañaque Doctors Hospital retains personal and sensitive personal information only for as long as necessary to fulfill the purposes for which it was collected, provide healthcare services, comply with legal and regulatory requirements, and satisfy applicable medical record retention periods under Philippine laws and hospital policies.
Upon expiration of the approved retention period, records are disposed of securely using methods appropriate to their format:
Manual (Paper-Based) Records
- Medical records, forms, and other paper documents containing personal data are securely destroyed through cross-cut shredding by authorized personnel or an accredited records disposal provider.
- Disposal is documented through a Records Disposal Log or Certificate of Destruction and is conducted with the approval of the authorized records custodian.
Electronic Records
- Personal data stored in the Hospital Information System (HIS), databases, servers, backup media, and other electronic devices are disposed of through secure deletion, data wiping or physical destruction of storage media when the media is no longer reusable.
- Electronic disposal is performed by the IT Department following hospital information security procedures to ensure that data cannot be reconstructed or recovered.
All disposal activities are carried out in a manner that preserves the confidentiality, integrity, and security of personal data and are subject to appropriate documentation and authorization.
Methods Used for Automated Access
PDH may use information systems and electronic processes in the collection, storage, retrieval, processing, and management of personal data.
Where automated processing or automated access is applicable, appropriate safeguards and access controls are implemented to protect personal data and limit access to authorized personnel.
Your Rights as a Data Subject
Under the Data Privacy Act, you have the right to:
- Be informed about the processing of your personal data;
- Access your personal data;
- Correct or update inaccurate or incomplete personal data;
- Object to certain processing;
- Withdraw consent when consent is the applicable basis for processing;
- Request the blocking, removal, or destruction of personal data in appropriate circumstances;
- Lodge a complaint concerning the processing of your personal data; and
- Exercise other rights provided under the Data Privacy Act and applicable regulations.
Contact the Data Protection Officer
For questions, concerns, or requests regarding your personal information or the exercise of your privacy rights, please contact:
PDH Data Protection Officer
Email: dpo@pdh.com.ph

Acknowledgment
By availing of the hospital’s services, submitting your personal information, or using our facilities, systems, or online services, you acknowledge that you have read and understood this Data Privacy Notice and that your personal information will be processed in accordance with applicable laws and the hospital’s privacy policies.
